- **evidence-synthesis**: 证据合成 skill。按研究方案合成证据：先判能不能做 meta 分析；能做的，干预类两两比较照 RevMan 的方法算（Mantel-Haenszel、倒方差、Peto，固定效应和随机效应），诊断准确性用双变量模型和汇总 ROC 曲线，用预测区域描述异质性，研究数够时做 Deeks 检验；不能做的，按 SWiM 做叙述性合成；范围综述只做证据图谱。用户说「合并分析」「meta 分析」「森林图」「证据合成」「synthesize」时使用。不用于没有经过提取和核对的数据，不下临床结论。 ([clarkchenkai/med-research](https://github.com/clarkchenkai/med-research/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-synthesis))
- **evidence-synthesis**: Turn a research question into a claim-by-source evidence matrix showing agreement, contradictions, and gaps. Use for evidence matrices, literature synthesis, research consensus or disagreements, supported vs contested claims, thesis evidence mapping, research gaps, or evidence tables with study design and sample size. Produce a cited Markdown synthesis report and a CSV matrix with one row per claim-source pair. Complements a formal systematic-review protocol; does not replace it. ([kwpledger/quick-references-and-skills](https://github.com/kwpledger/quick-references-and-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-synthesis))

Provides structured methodology for analyzing agreement, disagreement, and confidence levels across multiple research papers. ([Mingel-github/claude-skills](https://github.com/Mingel-github/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/med-evidence-synthesis))
